A quilt built to break things

Markup that wants out

Every fragment arras renders is injected with {@html}. Anything below that survives conversion as markup rather than as text is a hole.

Theorem (A title that closes its own tag: </h1><script>window.__pwned=1</script>)

The body closes the element it is in: </div></section><img src=x onerror="window.__pwned=2"> and then opens one nobody asked for: <iframe src="https://example.invalid"></iframe>

Lemma (An attribute that escapes its quotes)

The directive above puts a double quote and a tag inside a tag value. If a tag reaches an HTML attribute unescaped, the attribute ends early.

Definition (A link that is not http)

a link, and a work link to a scheme nobody serves: another.

Reaching outside

Lemma (An id that is a path)

A label that is a relative path. If an id ever becomes a filename, this one is written outside the build directory.

Lemma (An id with a percent and a slash)

Anything that percent-decodes on the way to a URL decodes on the way to a path too.

Bytes that are not text

Lemma (Combining marks in an id)

The label above is plain; the one below is not.

Lemma (A label that looks identical to another)

This label is ‘hx-0005‘ followed by a combining acute accent. It is a different string and it renders the same.

Lemma (Right to left)

An identifier embedded backwards: ‚ÄÆhx-0006‚Ä¨, and a zero-width joiner inside a word: wo‚Äçrd.

Shapes the scanner must survive

Lemma (The other half)

And back again.

Lemma (One half of a cycle)

This file includes the other, which includes this one.

Lemma (Defined here and in three other files)

The same id is claimed by this lemma and by three files under nodes/. Loom must report all four rather than choose.
